                \Huge \textbf{Dichlorobenzene} \\[1cm]
                \includegraphics[width=0.5\textwidth]{dichlorobenzene} \\[0.5cm]
                \Large Point Group: \textbf{D2h}
            \end{center}\newpage\section*{p orbitals}
Symmetry behavior of the p-orbitals:
$$\mathsf{\Gamma}_{red} = \,8 E\;\oplus{}\,-4 C2(y)\;\oplus{}\,-8 \sigma (xy)\;\oplus{}\,4 \sigma (yz)\;$$
$$\mathsf{\Gamma}_{irred} = \,1 B2g\;\oplus{}\,3 B3g\;\oplus{}\,1 Au\;\oplus{}\,3 B1u\;$$


\begin{itemize}
\item SALC of B3g: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{p}_{1} - \text{p}_{4}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of B1u: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{p}_{1} + \text{p}_{4}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of B2g: \quad $\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B3g: \quad $\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of Au: \quad $\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B1u: \quad $\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B3g: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{p}_{7} - \text{p}_{8}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of B1u: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{p}_{7} + \text{p}_{8}\right)}{2}$
\end{itemize}
Put together these SALCs form the following Hamilton matrices:
     $$ H_{B3g}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha} & \sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta} & 0\\\sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta} & \tilde{\alpha} - \tilde{\beta} & 0\\0 & 0 & \tilde{\alpha}_{Cl}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{B1u}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha} & \sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta} & 2 \tilde{\beta}_{Cl}\\\sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta} & \tilde{\alpha} + \tilde{\beta} & 0\\2 \tilde{\beta}_{Cl} & 0 & \tilde{\alpha}_{Cl}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{B2g}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha} + \tilde{\beta}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{Au}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha} - \tilde{\beta}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 


Solving Hückels secular equations, that follow from these H, leads to:

SALC in B3g}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{6}}{3} & \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} & 0\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{3} \left(2 \text{p}_{1} + \text{p}_{2} - \text{p}_{3} - 2 \text{p}_{4} - \text{p}_{5} + \text{p}_{6}\right)}{6}

\left[\begin{matrix}0 & 0 & 1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in B3g} \\ \text{2. SALC in B3g} \\ \text{3. SALC in B3g}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}0 & 0 & 1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{p}_{7} - \text{p}_{8}\right)}{2} 
 $$ 
 )

\item orbital of B3g with energy = 
                                $\tilde{\alpha} - 2 \tilde{\beta}$ \\
( 
$$
\left[\begin{matrix}- \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} & \frac{\sqrt{6}}{3} & 0\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in B3g} \\ \text{2. SALC in B3g} \\ \text{3. SALC in B3g}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}- \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} & \frac{\sqrt{6}}{3} & 0\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{p}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{6} \left(- \text{p}_{1} + \text{p}_{2} - \text{p}_{3} + \text{p}_{4} - \text{p}_{5} + \text{p}_{6}\right)}{6} 
 $$ 
 )

\item orbital of B2g with energy = 
                                $\tilde{\alpha} + \tilde{\beta}$ \\
( 
$$
\left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in B2g}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2} 
 $$ 
 )

\item orbital of Au with energy = 
                                $\tilde{\alpha} - \tilde{\beta}$ \\
( 
$$
\left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in Au}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\text{p}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{p}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{p}_{6}}{2} 
 $$ 
 )

\end{itemize}



\newpage \section*{s orbitals}
Symmetry behavior of the s-orbitals:
$$\mathsf{\Gamma}_{red} = \,8 E\;\oplus{}\,4 C2(y)\;\oplus{}\,8 \sigma (xy)\;\oplus{}\,4 \sigma (yz)\;$$
$$\mathsf{\Gamma}_{irred} = \,3 Ag\;\oplus{}\,1 B1g\;\oplus{}\,3 B2u\;\oplus{}\,1 B3u\;$$


\begin{itemize}
\item SALC of Ag: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{s}_{1} + \text{s}_{4}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of B2u: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{s}_{1} - \text{s}_{4}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of Ag: \quad $\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B1g: \quad $\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B2u: \quad $\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of B3u: \quad $\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}$
\item SALC of Ag: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{s}_{7} + \text{s}_{8}\right)}{2}$
\item SALC of B2u: \quad $\frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{s}_{7} - \text{s}_{8}\right)}{2}$
\end{itemize}
Put together these SALCs form the following Hamilton matrices:
     $$ H_{Ag}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha}_{s} & \sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta}_{s} & 2 beta_{\text{s}_Cl}\\\sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta}_{s} & \tilde{\alpha}_{s} + \tilde{\beta}_{s} & 0\\2 beta_{\text{s}_Cl} & 0 & alpha_{\text{s}_Cl}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{B2u}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha}_{s} & \sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta}_{s} & 0\\\sqrt{2} \tilde{\beta}_{s} & \tilde{\alpha}_{s} - \tilde{\beta}_{s} & 0\\0 & 0 & alpha_{\text{s}_Cl}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{B1g}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha}_{s} - \tilde{\beta}_{s}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 
     $$ H_{B3u}= \left[\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{\alpha}_{s} + \tilde{\beta}_{s}\end{bmatrix}\right]$$ 


Solving Hückels secular equations, that follow from these H, leads to:

SALC in B2u}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{6}}{3} & \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} & 0\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{3} \left(2 \text{s}_{1} + \text{s}_{2} - \text{s}_{3} - 2 \text{s}_{4} - \text{s}_{5} + \text{s}_{6}\right)}{6}

( 
$$
\left[\begin{matrix}0 & 0 & 1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in B2u} \\ \text{2. SALC in B2u} \\ \text{3. SALC in B2u}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}0 & 0 & 1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{2} \left(\text{s}_{7} - \text{s}_{8}\right)}{2} 
 $$ 
 )

                                $\tilde{\alpha}_{s} - 2 \tilde{\beta}_{s}$ \\

$$

SALC in B2u}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}- \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} & \frac{\sqrt{6}}{3} & 0\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{1}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{4}}{2}\\\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}\\\frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{7}}{2} - \frac{\sqrt{2} \text{s}_{8}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\sqrt{6} \left(- \text{s}_{1} + \text{s}_{2} - \text{s}_{3} + \text{s}_{4} - \text{s}_{5} + \text{s}_{6}\right)}{6} 
 $$ 
 )

\item orbital of B3u with energy = 
                                $\tilde{\alpha}_{s} + \tilde{\beta}_{s}$ \\
( 
$$

 $$ $$ = \frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2} 
 $$ 
 )

\item orbital of B1g with energy =

( 
$$
\left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{array}{c}\text{1. SALC in B1g}\end{array}\right] = \left[\begin{matrix}1\end{matrix}\right] * \left[\begin{matrix}\frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2}\end{matrix}\right] 
 $$ $$ = \frac{\text{s}_{2}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{3}}{2} + \frac{\text{s}_{5}}{2} - \frac{\text{s}_{6}}{2} 
 $$ 
 )
